# Third-party notices

This file covers the locked Windows x64 Rust normal dependency graph and npm production packages used to build Remova. It includes notices for the Microsoft WebView2 loader linked by webview2-com-sys.

License expressions are quoted from upstream package metadata. The links below point to the complete license and notice texts from each package or its versioned upstream source. Identical texts are printed once, with line endings normalized.

Regenerate from the repository root after installing the locked dependencies with `npm ci` and `cargo fetch --locked --manifest-path src-tauri/Cargo.toml`: `node scripts/make-sbom.mjs --notices THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md`.

## Windows installer components

- Tauri CLI 2.12.3 NSIS installer template (NSIS) — MIT. [`LICENSE-MIT`](#text-9dd42ea92cff2ede5cd477cbfcce051b2d0115c0ac7f368ee88cb545055dff1d) ([upstream](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tauri-apps/tauri/tauri-cli-v2.12.3/LICENSE-MIT)). [Corresponding source](https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/blob/tauri-cli-v2.12.3/crates/tauri-bundler/src/bundle/windows/nsis/installer.nsi).
- NSIS 3.13 runtime stub, LZMA module and plugins (NSIS) — zlib/libpng and CPL-1.0 with the LZMA exception. [`COPYING`](#text-6cc665347be5c84b76bf0fd6f85fbb0c33261e89b3408aa3e3e55dbe95e12d89) ([upstream](https://github.com/tauri-apps/binary-releases/releases/download/nsis-3.13/nsis-3.13.zip)). [Corresponding source](https://sourceforge.net/projects/nsis/files/NSIS%203/3.13/nsis-3.13-src.tar.bz2/download).
- nsis_tauri_utils 0.5.3 (NSIS) — Apache-2.0 OR MIT. [`LICENSE_MIT`](#text-1c1020fa10a6bf318717e82c911bcc54ebdfb9bb280460ae332bcb2f82f57fbe) ([upstream](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tauri-apps/nsis-tauri-utils/nsis_tauri_utils-v0.5.3/LICENSE_MIT)), [`LICENSE_APACHE-2.0`](#text-0d542e0c8804e39aa7f37eb00da5a762149dc682d7829451287e11b938e94594) ([upstream](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tauri-apps/nsis-tauri-utils/nsis_tauri_utils-v0.5.3/LICENSE_APACHE-2.0)). [Corresponding source](https://github.com/tauri-apps/nsis-tauri-utils/tree/nsis_tauri_utils-v0.5.3).
- WiX Toolset 3.14.1 UI resources and custom action (WixUIWixca) (MSI) — MS-RL. [`LICENSE.TXT`](#text-dfdf2048787635215a6baf3b9d461dee89a2904d246787258a44f072a98d4786) ([upstream](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/wixtoolset/wix3/b40e9a32c24033e11b77baf2c91a704382f898ed/LICENSE.TXT)). [Corresponding source](https://github.com/wixtoolset/wix3/tree/b40e9a32c24033e11b77baf2c91a704382f898ed).

The MSI and NSIS installers also include the Microsoft Edge WebView2 Evergreen Bootstrapper for online runtime installation. [Microsoft WebView2 distribution and downloads](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-edge/webview2/concepts/distribution).

### Text b3d734001a94efff3579978d953391aa7115f877657d25eb54037a43875d078a

```text
*ring* uses an "ISC" license, like BoringSSL used to use, for new code
files. See LICENSE-other-bits for the text of that license.

See LICENSE-BoringSSL for code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the
Apache 2.0 license. Some code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the ISC
license. In each case, the license info is at the top of the file.

for the license to code that was sourced from the once_cell project.
```

## 2. Conditions for Sharing Data

long as the Data Recipient makes available the text of this agreement
with the shared Data.

## 3. No Restrictions on Results

with respect to the use, modification, or sharing of Results.

## 5. Definitions

this agreement.

5.2. "Data Provider" means any person who is the source of Data
provided under this agreement and in reliance on a Data Recipient's
agreement to its terms.

5.3. "Data Recipient" means any person who receives Data directly
or indirectly from a Data Provider and agrees to the terms of this
agreement.

5.4. "Results" means any outcome obtained by computational analysis
of Data, including for example machine learning models and models'
insights.
